# Read-only source investigation and evidence correction plan

**Scope:** all 122 `COMPLETE` rows covered by [semantic_audit_report.md](./semantic_audit_report.md): 115 `FAIL`, 7 `NEEDS_REVIEW`. For every case this report inspects exact cited source IDs/files, request identity and history, eligible subject-linked records, relevant explicitly linked activity, and applicable deletion rules. This is an investigation plan only. No statuses, source data, output, validator, or ZIP were changed.

**Evidence standard:** ownership requires an identifier and resource in the same source record or correlated trace. Timestamps/proximity and inferred account IDs do not prove ownership. Activity/workflow results are claims. Log starts have no state; terminal results do. One lookup does not prove the global copy inventory. Prior PASS/FAIL/NEEDS_REVIEW classifications are carried over as audit labels, not newly adjudicated here.

**Source findings:** all 122 cases were reviewed. Of 25 earlier policy-conflict flags, 22 have direct, eligible request-email/resource traces ending in rows=0/HTTP 404 (the apparent PRESENT is a lookup-start parsing issue). `case_390` has a later eligible NOT_FOUND result in a linked JSON snapshot. `case_546` and `case_548` have actual identifier-bearing tombstones requiring rule_15 redaction. Extra foreign-resource steps also occur in `case_196`, `case_374`, and `case_79`.

## The 25 prior policy-conflict cases

The prior report listed: case_73, case_79, case_80, case_86, case_113, case_117, case_121, case_164, case_167, case_170, case_193, case_195, case_196, case_202, case_239, case_283, case_323, case_327, case_374, case_375, case_378, case_390, case_486, case_546, case_548. Source review resolves 22 cases by terminal request-email-linked zero-row/404 lookup evidence; `case_390` is resolved by its later cutoff-eligible NOT_FOUND snapshot. The two confirmed direct-identifier tombstone violations are `case_546` and `case_548` (`evidence_52`, rule_15). Cases `case_196`, `case_374`, and `case_79` also contain extra resources keyed to other subjects; their correct target traces are separately present. This source review supersedes the earlier generic ?PRESENT? interpretation for terminal lookup cases, but does not prove complete deletion.

## Resolvable from existing evidence vs. new evidence required

- **Existing source evidence can correct interpretation:** 22 profile traces show request-email/resource-linked completion with rows=0/404; case_390 has a later, subject-linked NOT_FOUND record. These establish only the observed lookup/snapshot at those resource/time points.
- **Existing evidence proves violations, not remediation:** evidence_52 shows direct identifiers on tombstones for user_546 and user_548. Redaction plus new eligible readback is still required.
- **Existing evidence proves correlation errors:** case_196, case_374, and case_79 include adjacent/foreign lookup keys. Keep only the target-subject trace/resource in any future candidate.
- **New evidence required where absent:** eligible explicit primary-profile absence or redacted tombstone; complete relevant-copy inventory; support shared-context redaction/attachment ownership; legal hold/retention basis; analytics anonymization; disabled active sync/import; and blocked backup restores. Do not infer any of these from a timestamp, workflow result, unrelated empty table, or proposed action.
- **No output produced:** this report is a planning artifact only; output.csv, statuses, datasets, validator, and code.zip were not changed.
